# Batch 38 — beat jpcorpwp: Japanese listed company sites that publish their news through
# the WordPress REST API.
# id|mode|want|category|record_type|tags|portal|name|name_ja|url|probe|description|opts
#
# HOW THIS BEAT WAS FOUND. Organisation lists, all fetched open data:
#   * 3,816 listed companies with 証券コード, 707 financial institutions, and
#     1,794 local governments — ymstmsys/japan-domain-list (CC BY 4.0).
#   * 1,741 municipalities with 総務省 団体コード + official URL —
#     nkawa/Convert-Local-Government-URLs.
#   * 47 prefectures — code4fukui/localgovjp.
#   * 572 Japanese universities with ac.jp domains — Hipo/university-domains-list.
# 6,720 hosts were probed for four conventions: CKAN package_search, WordPress
# REST posts, ArcGIS service directory, OAI-PMH Identify. Only WordPress REST
# answered at any scale — 465 live of 6,720 hosts. CKAN, ArcGIS and OAI returned
# nothing at these hostnames, and 7,152 DERIVED open-data hostnames
# (opendata./data./odcs./catalog. on each real domain) answered 4 times.
# The negative result is recorded in docs/jp-endpoint-discovery-2026-10.md so the
# next author does not spend 13,872 requests rediscovering it.
#
# Every row below returned real posts on 2026-10-06; 3 hosts already present in
# native/collectors/ were dropped. Counts are first-page counts.
